Nine ledger rules tested by `tests/unit/roadmap-check.test.ts` **17** against synthetic ledgers that break exactly one rule each, plus a positive control that reads this file. Installing the hook is `npm run hooks:install`, which a human runs because it changes git config (AGENTS.md §5). "Green on the default branch" is **F-12**'s done-when, not this one, and is still `[~]` until an Actions run is observed. _2026-11-03 Qoder agent (T-01/T-02):_ a defect in the gate itself, found by using it: `--write` recomputed the dashboard and then **still exited 1** quoting "§1 dashboard does not match", because the problem list was built from the bytes it had just replaced — so `roadmap:check -- --write && git add ROADMAP.md` could never chain, and a successful repair looked like a failure. Fixed by splitting ledger problems (re-read after the write) from repository problems (a commit that skipped ROADMAP.md is not repairable by writing, so those survive the re-read). Proven in both directions rather than assumed: a deliberately wrong phase row → `--write` exits **0** and a following plain check exits 0; an `[x]` item stripped of its `Evidence:` line → `--write` exits **1** naming line 134, so the repair path did not become a bypass.
- [x] **F-12** CI workflow: lint, typecheck, test, `roadmap:check`. _Deps:_ F-01, F-11. _Evidence:_ **GitHub Actions reports green on `main`: run [41665657293](https://github.com/sightline-team/sightline/actions/runs/41665657293), `event: push`, `head_sha: 1d8da18`, job `checks` `conclusion: success` in 72 s (2026-11-03 15:36:34Z → 15:37:46Z), every step `success` — Install (lockfile only), Lint, Typecheck, Test (unit, verification, relay), Roadmap gate (F-11).** The run was made possible by B-09 being re-measured (the token is live), and it checked the four commits that had been sitting local, so the gate was exercised against a real multi-commit range on a real runner rather than a synthetic one. `.github/workflows/ci.yml` was written in the P-06 chunk and its range selection was executed against all four payload shapes before this; what was missing was a runner, and there is one now. Playwright remains **deliberately out** (see **B-08**: `npx playwright install` plus the `localhost` host question, and **A-02** owns production-build e2e — 33 e2e tests pass locally, and CI does not claim them), and so do `spike`/`golden`, which need a key (**B-05**). This file's §1 no longer says "on its first Actions run": it has run, and it is green.
- [x] **F-13** `.gitignore` (`.env*`, build output), `server-only` guard on `lib/server` and `app/api`. _Done when:_ importing server code into a client component fails the build. _Evidence:_ a temporary `src/app/probe/page.tsx` marked `'use client'` that imported `serverConfig` made `npx next build` exit **1** with `You're importing a module that depends on "server-only"` and the chain `./src/lib/server/env.ts [Client Component Browser] ← ./src/app/probe/page.tsx`; the probe was then deleted and the build returned to exit 0. First probe attempt in `src/app/_probe/` proved nothing (an underscore folder is a Next private folder, never compiled) — that is why the route was re-made routable. `.gitignore` covers `.env`, `.env.*` with `!.env.example`. `app/api/*` route handlers will import the same guard when F-05 creates them.
- [x] **F-14** Structural scaffold: the whole module tree with typed stubs that **refuse**, so scope is visible before features are built. _Requested by the human 2026-11-02._ _Done when:_ every file `docs/PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md` §1 names exists; each unfinished function throws `NotBuilt` naming its owning item; route handlers answer 501 and forward nothing; placeholder pages say which item builds them; no stub returns a plausible fake. _Evidence:_ 60 new files plus 9 modified (`LICENSE`, `README.md`, `package.json`, `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`, `docs/ARCHITECTURE_ESSENTIALS.md`, `docs/PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md`, `src/app/layout.tsx`, `src/app/page.tsx`, `src/lib/config.ts`) counted with `git ls-files --others --exclude-standard | wc -l` and `git diff --name-only`, not recalled — `src/types`, `src/lib/{errors,not_built,image,session,a11y,fixtures,server,near-ai}`, `src/components`, `src/features/{capture,analysis,qa,speech,trust}`, `src/app/{read,trust,settings,api/verify,api/relay}`, `scripts/{spike,record-fixtures,run-golden,roadmap-check,crosscheck}.ts`, `fixtures/{responses,attestation}/README.md`, `public/{manifest.webmanifest,icons/icon.svg,sample-docs/README.md}`, `docs/{USER_RESEARCH,ACCESSIBILITY_REPORT}.md`, `tests/{unit,relay,verification,e2e,a11y}`, README + LICENSE. Stub discipline proved: `tests/verification/attestation.test.ts` (11 passing) calls all six trust-path stubs and asserts each throws `NotBuilt` with the right item ID, and refuses the alternative — "returned a value instead of refusing"; `tests/relay/relay.test.ts` (5) proves both API routes return 501, name their item, forward nothing and leak no key. `npx tsc --noEmit` 0, `npx eslint .` 0 errors, `npx vitest run` **138 passed | 9 todo** in 9 files, `npx next build` 0 with routes `/ /read /trust /settings /api/verify /api/relay/[...path]`. Two scaffold decisions recorded in code comments: nav uses `next/link` because a full page load would discard the in-memory-only session, and `SpeakButton` is placeholder **text** rather than an inert button because a control that does nothing is an accessibility bug.

### Phase U — User research (parallel; first session by 2026-11-05)

- [ ] **U-01** Find at least one blind or low-vision person willing to talk (through your network or community organisations). _Done when:_ a session is scheduled.
- [ ] **U-02** Hold the session with consent. Ask: how do you handle sensitive mail today, what would you trust, is explanation or reading more useful, what would make you stop using this. _Done when:_ notes in `docs/USER_RESEARCH.md` (no personal documents recorded).
- [ ] **U-03** Update PRD personas, §2 and §12 from findings; revise scope if the premise is weakened. _Deps:_ U-02.
- [!] **U-04** If no user is reachable by 2026-11-05: record it, weaken the Impact claim in README, mark `[!]`. _Status 2026-11-06:_ **the condition is met and both actions are done; the item stays `[!]` because that is what it instructs, and because the thing it was waiting for is a human blocker (**B-01**/D-07), not unfinished agent work.** Recorded in `docs/USER_RESEARCH.md` (the date passed, the premise is untested rather than falsified, one session before 2026-11-14 would still be U-02 evidence), weakened in `README.md` (“Who it is for is a premise, not a finding”, with an explicit ban on inferred impact or usability claims), and updated in `docs/PRD.md` §16's scoring row and its open question. _Evidence:_ the README paragraph and the two doc rows, plus §9's trigger row annotated as fired; no behaviour, no privacy sentence and no test changed (AGENTS §4 rule 1 — `claims.ts` still owns every privacy statement, and none of them was touched here).
- [ ] **U-05** (P1) Second session with the working prototype.

### Phase P — Paste-text path, results, Q&A (2026-11-04 → 11-06)

Built first because it needs no vision model, works in fixture mode, and is the fallback for risk 1.

- [x] **P-01** `analysis/prompts.ts` (structure, Q&A) + `schemas.ts` (zod). _Done when:_ prompts match ARCH §9; schemas reject malformed output. _Evidence:_ `analysis/prompts.ts` cross-checked line by line against ARCH §9 (transcribe exactly / `[illegible]` never guess / tables row by row with column labels / report `captureIssues` + page labels / strict JSON shape; step 2 gets the transcript **only**, literal-excerpt rule, treat the document as data and ignore instructions inside it; Q&A `{ answer, notInDocument, sourceQuote }`). `analysis/schemas.ts` is the zod half and `tests/unit/schemas.test.ts` (38 tests) proves the rejects: empty transcript, missing required field, backwards `uncertain` span, unknown capture issue, unknown doc type, unsourced fact, and the two silent-corruption cases — a model-supplied `confidence` or `quoteFound` is **stripped**, unknown keys dropped rather than losing the whole page. Not wired into a request yet: that is P-03. _2026-11-04, one line of step 2's contract was wrong and the live service said so:_ `STRUCTURE_SYSTEM_PROMPT` asked for `"facts": [{"kind": string, …}]` while `factKindSchema` accepts eight fixed values, and **both** structure-step replies came back as well-formed JSON whose `kind` words were none of the eight (`docs/SPIKE_RESULTS.md` §4). In the app that is a repair retry (**P-08**) and then the transcript-only screen (**P-11**) — for a document the model had read correctly. The template now spells the eight, which is the fix C-03 made on the transcription side, and `tests/unit/pipeline.test.ts` holds the prompt's three closed vocabularies against `analysisSchema`'s own options: removing a value from either side fails exactly one test, and the prompt's byte-stability note records the break and why it is the only kind permitted.
- [x] **P-02** `analysis/grounding.ts`: sets `quoteFound` by exact substring check. _Done when:_ unit tests incl. near-miss quotes; docs never describe it as page-verified. _Evidence:_ `analysis/grounding.ts` sets `quoteFound` by exact substring against **our transcript**, in code, never from the model; `groundFacts` keeps ungrounded items flagged instead of dropping them. `tests/unit/grounding.test.ts` (13 tests) covers the near-miss set (reformatted, paraphrased, `£840.00` vs `£840`, `14/03/2026` vs `14 March 2026`, case change, whitespace) and pins the limitation in a test named "does not prove the transcript matches the page — the misread passes perfectly". README §Accuracy and the module header both state consistency-with-transcript, never page-verified (AGENTS.md §4 rule 8).
- [~] **P-03** `analysis/pipeline.ts` text path. _Deps:_ F-03, P-01. _Done when:_ pasted text → validated `Analysis` in fixture mode and real mode. _2026-11-02 Qoder agent:_ **the module is written and its ordering is proven; the two modes it must run end-to-end do not exist yet.** `runPipeline` (text path) does: normalise the pasted transcript once → gate → build the body only after the gate passes → one `chat/completions` call → verify the signature from the exact bytes F-03 returned → emit the `TrustRecord` → discard the analysis if the signature is unverified → zod-parse and ground. `tests/unit/pipeline.test.ts` (21 tests, transport/gate/verifier all fakes) asserts call *order* as the privacy property: a failed gate leaves `posts` empty and the log at `['onTranscript','gate']`; a rejected signature still emits the receipt before halting; `sentBody`/`rawText` reach the verifier byte-identical, trailing `\n\n` included (ADR-19); an instruction written inside the document stays inside the fenced user message and the system message is byte-stable (AGENTS.md §11); Mode F runs no gate and no signature check and lands on `claim: 'fixtures'` while still showing an answer. Image input throws `NotBuilt('C-05')`. **Still open, and the reason this is `[~]` not `[x]`:** the done-when names both modes, and neither can run for real yet — Mode F needs F-09's replay client (`createNearClient` still throws `NotBuilt('F-09')`), real mode needs T-01/T-02's gate and verifier plus a live response (B-05). What is proven here is the sequence and the fail-closed consequences, which is exactly the part that cannot be checked later by looking at a screen. _2026-11-03 update (same agent):_ the Mode F half of the done-when **now runs end to end in a browser** — `src/app/_flow/useDocumentRun.ts` calls `runPipeline` with `createFixtureClient()`, and `tests/e2e/flow.spec.ts` completes a run and reads the `Analysis` on screen with no key. The remaining half is unchanged: real mode still needs T-01/T-02 and a live response (B-05), which is why this stays `[~]`.
- [x] **P-04** `ResultsView`: Summary → Key facts (with quotes) → Actions → Warnings → Full text → Ask → Trust receipt; "Skip to full text". _Done when:_ heading order verified by an e2e test. _Evidence:_ `tests/e2e/flow.spec.ts` asserts `main h2` **as an array** — `['Summary','Key facts','What you may need to do','Warnings','Full text','Ask a question','Verification']` — for a real completed run in the browser, so the receipt cannot drift above the answer. `src/features/analysis/ResultsView.tsx` shows the doc type in plain words, a "Skip to full text" anchor to `#full-text`, each key fact as `<dt>`/`<dd>` with its quote or the flag "— Sightline could not find these words in the text it was given" (§4 rule 8: flagged, never sourced; kept, never dropped), actions with deadlines, warnings, the transcript the user pasted in `<p class="transcript">`, an Ask section that says the question box is not built rather than showing an inert input, then `TrustReceipt`. Focus lands on the `<h1>` after navigation (asserted with `toBeFocused`). Proved against all three demo documents, including the authored misquote. axe: 0 serious/critical on the completed view.
- [x] **P-05** Home page: paste-text input. _Done when:_ keyboard- and screen-reader-operable. _Evidence:_ `src/features/paste/PasteView.tsx` + `src/lib/session/store.tsx` + `src/app/_flow/useDocumentRun.ts`, driven by `tests/e2e/flow.spec.ts` (13 tests) in fixture mode with no key. Keyboard: `Tab` reaches `#document-text` then the submit control with nothing between them, typing then `Enter` on that button completes a run; a real `<label for>`, a native `<textarea>` and `aria-describedby` hint; the single announcer carries every status change ("Reading the demo text.", the demo-document handover sentence, "There is nothing in the text box yet.", "Done. Showing what Sightline read.", and an error's own `userMessage`); after a failure focus moves to the error summary (`StatusMessage` is the one focusable node, via `ref` as a prop). Session state lives in the reducer behind `SessionProvider`, mounted in `layout.tsx` under `AnnouncerProvider`, so client navigation between `/`, `/read` and `/trust` keeps the document — asserted by a test that runs a document, leaves, and comes back to find the transcript still there. **Screen-reader-operable is claimed only as far as the DOM contract**: A-03/A-04 (a person with a reader) are still open, and the finding that Next.js adds its own live region on client navigation is recorded in `docs/ACCESSIBILITY_REPORT.md` rather than smoothed over. Live modes (relay/direct) refuse at the door with `model_unavailable` naming T-01/T-02 and the missing allowlist, so nothing on screen can imply a verified run before verification exists.
- [x] **P-06** `qa/ask.ts` + `AskPanel`: transcript-only answers, `notInDocument`, `quoteFound`. Disclose that OS dictation is handled by the OS. _Done when:_ 5 prepared questions on 3 docs answered correctly or correctly refused. _Evidence:_ **10 prepared questions across the 3 corpus documents**, each resolving to its honest state in `tests/unit/fixtures.test.ts` › `every prepared question in the corpus resolves to its honest state` — a table that runs the **real** pipeline against the **real** replay client with the gate and the verifier set to throw if either is called, and checks every `sourceQuote` against the document text itself (`fixture.text.includes(reply.sourceQuote)`). 9 answered, 1 authored near-miss flagged `quoteFound: false`, 1 honest `notInDocument`; refusal paths covered too (`no_fixture_for_question` for a real question in the wrong document, `no_fixture_for_input` for a prepared wording asked of a non-demo text). `tests/unit/qa.test.ts` **26** (order: transcript → gate → question → signature → receipt, with the receipt emitted *before* the signature halt so a discarded answer still shows; byte-exact `sentBody`/`rawText` into the verifier per ADR-19; the document's own instructions kept inside the fenced user message and never in the system prompt; zero GETs of its own). `tests/e2e/flow.spec.ts` **21** including six Q&A tests in a browser with no key (ask → answer → flagged answer → "the document does not answer this" → a refusal that focuses the error summary → keyboard-only asking) plus an axe sweep with answers on the page. Dictation is disclosed as an OS function in the `aria-describedby` hint, not claimed as an app feature. One bug found and fixed while wiring this: `sessionReducer`'s `question_answered` kept the previous error, so a refused question stayed on screen above the next answer — it now clears `error` and derives `status` from whether an analysis exists (`tests/unit/session-reducer.test.ts` **17**, two of them this).
- [x] **P-07** Safety notices (medication, dose, legal deadline, money); no scam-detection claim; English-only message; urgency caution. _Done when:_ copy reviewed for plain language. _Evidence:_ `src/features/safety/notices.ts` writes four notices and computes which apply from **fields of the validated `Analysis`** — `docType`, a fact's `kind`, an action's `deadline` — never by hunting the transcript for "£" or "tablet", because that would be a second unvalidated reader inside the app whose misses nobody sees (cost stated in a test: a health letter whose facts were filed `other` fires nothing). Rendered as **Sightline's own list**, then the model's sentences under "What the reading flagged", *inside* the existing Warnings section, so AGENTS.md §8's fixed order and the `main h2` array are unchanged. Copy: `PRD.md` §6 R7's "confirm with a pharmacist, doctor or the sender" is the medicine notice ("…does not give medical advice. Check with your doctor, a pharmacist or the surgery before you change anything"); the money notice carries §12's no-detection rule as a stated limit — "Sightline reads every letter the same way, so it cannot tell you whether this one is genuine… use contact details you already have" — and the words _scam_, _fraud_, _detect_ appear nowhere; the urgency caution is the deadline notice ("A date can be misread, so check it with the sender before you rely on it"); the English-only line is unconditional because it is about Sightline, not the document. **19 tests** in `tests/unit/safety-notices.test.ts`: firing and non-firing per notice, fixed order and no duplicates, determinism and no mutation of the reading, plus the copy held to measurable rules — ≤ 3 sentences and ≤ 20 words each, no jargon (`TEE`/attestation/enclave/encrypt/nonce/model), no dose, amount or date of our own, no privacy or data-flow sentence (rule 1 stays `claims.ts`'s), never the model's words repeated — and the three corpus documents run through the pipeline's own conversion (`analysisSchema.parse` → `groundFacts`) to show each demo fires exactly its justified set. **6 e2e tests** in `tests/e2e/flow.spec.ts` assert the sentences on screen in a browser with no key, that the two lists stay separate, and that the section order did not move; axe over a completed run: 0 serious/critical (39 e2e pass). The old e2e assertion that said "this version does not yet add its own safety notices" was replaced, deliberately, with counts for both lists. **What "reviewed" means here, stated rather than implied:** the copy was reviewed against `PRD.md` §6 R7/§7 and AGENTS.md §8 and the limits are machine-checked; it has **not** been read aloud by a blind or low-vision person (**U-01**/**A-03**, blocker **B-01**), which is the review that could still change these words.
- [x] **P-08** Error paths: invalid JSON (one repair retry, then transcript only), `finish_reason: length`, model refusal → offer paste-text. _Done when:_ unit tests for each. _Evidence:_ **18 new unit tests across the two call sites — 12 in `tests/unit/pipeline.test.ts` (now 33) › `the repair retry (P-08, ARCH §13)` + `truncation and refusal (P-08, ARCH §13)`, 6 in `tests/unit/qa.test.ts` (now 32) › `the repair retry and the named error paths (P-08)` — plus 3 in `tests/unit/session-reducer.test.ts` (now 20)** for the degraded state. The sequence moved to `src/lib/near-ai/completion.ts` (`readCompletion`): gate → body → signature → `TrustRecord` → read, with **one** repair retry when the content is not the JSON that was asked for. What each test pins: a retry re-gates and re-verifies (log `['onTranscript','gate','post','signature','onTrustRecord','gate','post','signature','onTrustRecord','onAnalysis']`, second verifier call carries the second attempt's bytes), the retry note joins the **user** message so both system prompts are byte-identical, the retry is spent once (two posts then a named failure, never an infinite loop), a truncation is **not** repaired (1 post, `output_truncated`), `finish_reason: 'stop'` and an absent `finish_reason` are never read as a pass, a refusal halts with `model_refused` and **never echoes the model's prose** (`userMessage` equals the app error's own), `refusal: null` is not a refusal. Consequences differ by call and each is tested: an analysis that cannot be read **degrades** to the transcript-only view (`run_degraded` → `Session.degraded`, `ResultsView` shows "No summary" and the sections that would have held the reading are **absent, not empty**; `tests/unit/session-reducer.test.ts` **20**, incl. that a degraded run is `ready` and not an error, and that a later successful read clears it), while a question **halts** on all three failure kinds because there is no transcript-only screen for an answer. `envelope.ts` now parses `finish_reason` and `message.refusal` as **optional** fields — both are `[Unverified]` (SPIKE §1 documents top-level response properties only), so absence is never a pass and confirming them is still S-08/S-11 (**B-05**). `buildRecord` is injected rather than imported because `src/lib/` must not import `src/features/` (AGENTS.md §7). **Honest limit:** Mode F cannot produce a truncation — every fixture reply is authored JSON — so the degraded screen is proved by unit tests and reducer state only, not in a browser; stated in ARCH §13. *(Corrected by **P-11**, 2026-11-03: the fourth corpus case replays prose rather than a truncation, which Mode F could not otherwise produce, so the screen now has 11 browser tests and an axe pass. A truncation still has no Mode F path and that half of this limit stands.)*
- [x] **P-09** `speech/SpeakButton`: one opt-in read-aloud button with rate; off by default. _Done when:_ no auto-play; stops on Forget. _Evidence:_ four pieces, each placed where it can be proved. **The engine call** — `src/features/speech/speak.ts` is real code now: `speak(text, { rate, synth, onDone })` takes the synthesiser as an argument because vitest runs in Node where neither `speechSynthesis` nor `SpeechSynthesisUtterance` exists, and says nothing rather than throwing when neither is available; `chunkForSpeech` hands the engine pieces of ≤ 200 characters, breaking at sentence ends and then at spaces, never between words unless it must, and loses no character of the input (asserted by rejoining the pieces, not by eyeballing them) — insurance against a reported "long utterance stops part-way" engine flaw that **[no device here has been listened to; A-03/A-04, B-01]**, cheap because `speechSynthesis.speak()` already queues. `stopSpeaking()` now goes through the *same* guard as `speak()`: the no-engine test found the old `'speechSynthesis' in window` check would have thrown inside a Forget press on a browser carrying the property with no engine behind it. **The rate** — `src/lib/settings.ts`: `localStorage['sightline.settings']`, zod-validated to 0.5–2 per ARCH §7, read afresh on every press so **A-15**'s control applies without a reload, and an unknown sibling field is *stripped rather than rejected* so Settings can grow beside it. Unparseable JSON, a string, `null`, `0.2`, `9`, `[]` all fall back to normal speed: a reading speed is not worth crashing the results screen over. **The button** — `SpeakButton.tsx` is a play/stop toggle on native HTML plus `aria-pressed` (`PRD.md` §6: "one button, play/stop"), labelled `Read the answer aloud` / `Stop reading` with the static sentence "Nothing is read until you press this."; it renders **nothing** where there is no engine, because a button that reads nothing aloud is the bug this file's own placeholder used to warn about; its unmount cleanup stops only speech *it* started (`started` ref), because React's StrictMode runs a mount → cleanup → mount cycle in development and an untouched control calling `cancel()` would contradict the sentence next to it. The placeholder is gone; the unused `isSpeaking()` scaffold export went with it. **What is spoken** — `app/_flow/ReadAloud.tsx` composes store + speech feature (features may not import each other, AGENTS.md §7) and `app/_flow/spoken_answer.ts` builds the words: **the `summary` field and `safetyNotices()` output only** — the two parts of the screen whose wording we control, taken from the same field and the same function the screen renders, so the audio cannot drift from the page or smuggle in a sentence nobody reviewed. The model's `warnings` array is deliberately **not** read: a listener has no way to tell whose sentence it is, and §13's whole point is that the two lists may disagree. Key facts, actions and the full text stay on the page for the reader or magnifier a person already runs — the scope `PRD.md` §12 Q1 row 9 left after voice was cut, stated here rather than discovered later. `ResultsView` takes a second slot and places the control **above** the first section, so §8's order is untouched. **24 new unit tests** (new file `tests/unit/speech.test.ts`) for chunking, order, rate, the single `onEnd`, the missing engine, and the settings validation path by path. **11 new e2e tests** (`tests/e2e/flow.spec.ts` › `Reading the answer aloud (P-09)`) with `window.speechSynthesis` replaced by a recorder installed before any app script runs: **zero utterances across a completed run and a route change** (the no-auto-play half of the done-when, and the off-by-default half); one press speaks the screen's own summary sentence and both cautions in ≤ 200-character pieces and flips label + `aria-pressed`; a second press cancels exactly once; the label reverts when the engine reports the end; a stored `speechRate: 1.8` reaches every utterance, and `not json at all` / `{"speechRate":9}` reach it as 1; **Forget cancels it** (the done-when's second half, which P-10 wired and this proves in a browser); no control appears where there is no answer or no engine; the spoken text matches no privacy phrase and never says "Verification"; and the `main h2` array is still the seven fixed sections with the control measured above them. The 44 px sweep already covers it — it walks every `main button` on the results stage, and the new one is in `main`. **Docs:** README §Accessibility says what the button reads, that it is not a screen reader, and the limit no check can derive — the engine belongs to the device and *some browser voices synthesise over the network*, so nothing anywhere claims reading aloud stays on the device (this is why `speak.ts`'s old "nothing here involves the network" comment was removed: it was a data-flow claim about code we do not own, and AGENTS §4 rule 1 forbids writing one); ARCH §12 carries the six as-built decisions, §6 and §7 the components and the settings key; ESSENTIALS, PROJECT_STRUCTURE and `docs/ACCESSIBILITY_REPORT.md` (rows 9–10 + a not-tested row) follow. Measured: `npx vitest run` **382 passed | 6 todo** in 18 files (was 358 | 6 in 17: **+24**, the one failing check being the dashboard until `roadmap:check -- --write`), `npx playwright test` **58 passed** = 12 shell + 46 flow (was 47: **+11**), `npx tsc --noEmit` and `npx eslint .` exit 0, `npx next build` exit 0 with the same six routes. **Honest limits:** no human has heard any of it — the tests prove the *calls*, not intelligibility, at speed, over a person's own reader (**A-03/A-04**, **B-01**); **nothing can change the rate yet**, because writing to `sightline.settings` is **A-15**'s job and only the read half exists; the 200-character cap is un-measured insurance; and the transcript-only screen (P-08) has **no** read-aloud control, since there is no summary to read there — noted on **P-11**, whose job it is to show that screen in a browser at all. *(**P-11** decided it on 2026-11-03, with the screen rendered: no control, proved by absence plus zero utterances reaching the recorder, for the reason in P-09's own full-text rule.)*
- [x] **P-10** `session/forget.ts` + UI: reset state, revoke object URLs, abort requests, clear session keys. _Done when:_ test confirms app state and storage hold no document data afterwards (README states what cannot be cleared). _Evidence:_ four pieces, split so each half is testable where it runs. **The pure half** — `src/lib/session/forget.ts`: `forgetSession(targets)` aborts → revokes → clears, in that order, attempts every target even when one throws, and returns `ForgetReport { aborted, revoked, cleared, failed }`; `sessionKeysOwnedBy` is the `sightline.` prefix rule. `tests/unit/forget.test.ts` **11 tests** (new file): every controller's `signal.aborted` flips; each URL is revoked with the exact string; only prefixed keys are ever handed to the remover and a foreign key is left in the store; the **abort happens before any clearing**, asserted as a call timeline (`['abort','revoke','remove']`) rather than as a comment; a throwing `revokeObjectUrl` still leaves the second URL and all three keys attempted with `failed: 1`; nothing registered means zeros and nothing called; the report holds numbers only, so no document-derived string can ride out of it into a log. **The wiring** — `SessionProvider.forget()` supplies the browser's own collaborators (`URL.revokeObjectURL`, `window.sessionStorage`) and dispatches `forgotten`; `src/app/_flow/useForget.ts` is the composition root that also calls `stopSpeaking()` (P-09's rule, and `src/lib/` may not import `src/features/`, AGENTS.md §7) and speaks the announcement from what the report counted. **The UI** — `src/components/ForgetButton.tsx` sits in the nav on every route, because the moment it exists for includes a run still going on the paste screen; `layout.tsx` now mounts the store over the nav to make that true. **The browser proof** — `tests/e2e/flow.spec.ts` **8 new tests**: the control is visible on all four routes; one press empties the screen, announces `Forgotten. Sightline has cleared the document and everything it read.`, moves focus to the heading of what is left and **does not navigate** (one press, one change); after a run with an answered question the bill's own words (`840`, `nc-2026-0441`, `care trust`, `amount due`, `invoice`) appear **nowhere in the body**; the words typed into the paste box leave the box too — that box is component state, so `PasteView` is now a keyed wrapper remounted on the new session id, because an effect that set state was both rejected by `react-hooks/set-state-in-effect` and would have rendered the old words once more first; the receipt returns to "Nothing has been verified yet" so the `TrustRecord`s went as well; a `sightline.` key really is gone from a real `sessionStorage` while an `other-tab.` key survives; the announced sentence contains no `enclave|encrypt|private|attest|verif|stored|sent|delete|remov|destroy` on any route; the control meets the 44 px rule. **README.md §Forgetting a document** is the honest statement the done-when asks for (it cannot recall what left the tab, cannot reach the device's copies — screen-reader speech history, clipboard, OS keyboard predictions, screenshots, browser history and autofill — and cannot bind a browser's back/forward cache), and `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` §5.4/§5.5 records the four decisions the spec left open. Measured: `npx vitest run` **358 passed | 6 todo** in 17 files (was 347 | 6 in 16), `npx playwright test` **47 passed** = 12 shell + 35 flow (was 39), `npx tsc --noEmit` and `npx eslint .` exit 0, `npx next build` exit 0 with the same six routes. **Honest limits:** no browser has ever watched Forget cancel a *slow* request, because Mode F answers instantly and live modes refuse at the door — that proof is **P-12**; and the object-URL half has no producer yet, so revocation is proved through the injected collaborator only until C-02 calls `trackObjectUrl`. *(Corrected by **P-12**, 2026-11-03: the press was made in a browser against a completion that was genuinely owed — both clicks in one task — and it found the first limit's worst case real. The transcript went out and the reading came back, because `runPipeline` reports through callbacks that fired after this item's end-of-run guard. `app/_flow/live_writes.ts` is the fix; the counted sentence that proved the state was reached also proved its grammar wrong ("the request that were still going"), which P-10 had written and nothing had ever spoken. What this item still does not own: a socket, because Mode F issues no request at all.*
- [x] **P-11** (new, from P-08) Prove the degraded view in a browser: a fourth corpus case whose reply is deliberately unreadable, so `run_degraded` and `ResultsView`'s "No summary" state run in fixture mode and sit inside A-01's axe sweep. _Done when:_ an e2e test reaches the transcript-only screen with no key. _Why this is an item and not a note:_ P-08's evidence says plainly that no browser has ever shown that screen — every fixture reply is authored JSON, so Mode F cannot truncate or mangle one. Adding a bad reply to the demo corpus is the only way to see it without **B-05**, and a screen a person reads aloud in a moment of failure is exactly the screen that should not be untested. _Also owed here (from P-07):_ the English-only notice, which is about Sightline rather than the document, should appear on that screen too — it was left out because `safetyNotices` computes from an `Analysis` and a degraded run has none, and a safety sentence no browser has ever displayed is a sentence nobody has checked. _Also owed here (from P-09):_ the transcript-only screen carries **no** read-aloud control, because the P-09 button reads the summary and Sightline's cautions and a degraded run has neither — deliberately not a control that says nothing. If that screen should offer the transcript itself read aloud, decide it here, with the screen rendered in front of you, rather than by adding a second kind of speech to the feature blind. _Evidence:_ **the screen is rendered, swept and asserted in a browser with no key** — `tests/e2e/flow.spec.ts` › `the transcript-only screen (P-11)`, **11 tests**, driven by a fourth corpus case (`ferney-bus-pass`, "Demo document 4: an answer that does not arrive") whose structure reply is prose twice over, so the real `readCompletion` retry, the real `run_degraded` dispatch and the real no-reading branch of `ResultsView` all execute.
